The abstract plan had met people with their own movements and fears.","The harbor engineer approached from the quay, carrying the cloth from the drain in a sealed bag. The quest for justice had become an excuse to stop asking whether his tactic endangered people.","Etta's sensitivity kept her from treating him as merely foolish. His sister had decided to emigrate after years of scarce work, and his anger had a history. That did not make it a reliable guide to every present claim. Psychology could illuminate the impulse without making the sabotage inevitable.","Mara looked back at the harbor model. Its curving paths had given form to a vision that physics and geology alone could not create. Imagination played a major role, as did the unglamorous skill of leaving a drain uncovered. Her realistic plan for the afternoon now included dry shoes.","Cassian offered his arm. \"Marital bliss?\"","\"A hot bath. after closing. Mara almost declined. She wanted a quiet dinner with her husband and a floor that did not threaten to submerge her shoes. Cassian read the invitation over her shoulder. \"There is cake.\"","\"You have an obsession with cake.\"","\"A useful character trait in a marriage.\"","They arrived to find the storm machine dry and silent. At the moment of their entrance, a little girl emerged carrying a dish of biscuits. She was the child from the quay. Her mother worked in the museum café, and she had decided that rescuers should be fed. Cassian knelt to accept one with elaborate gratitude. Mara had seen him face angry committees with less solemnity.","Etta's apology had already been published. The revised display credited the ingredient study by name and linked to a readable copy. A lawyer had reviewed the ownership records; the credit belonged there independently of the theft. Lio had contributed a short account of his sister's working life. It said nothing about his recent actions. Those were being handled through the ordinary investigation, without a performance arranged for visitors. The exit walk would become an integral part of every rehearsal.","\"He asked whether I could promise forgiveness,\" Etta said. \"I said I could promise to listen.\"","Mara thought that a realistic beginning. She did not want a sentimental ending to paralyze the practical work left to do. The blocked drain needed a barrier against loose cloth, and the display needed an operator who could see its outlet. Good intentions could not substitute for a robust physical arrangement.","The programmer joined them with a small paper diagram. The site-licensing contract had allowed the image kiosks, while the pump's controls remained separate. An expired token had silenced the projected harbor and distracted the attendant; it had never disabled the emergency switch. The distinction would now appear in staff training. Rafe's claim that the museum could be remotely held hostage had sounded plausible because nobody had explained its limits.","Mara listened, then asked a visitor's question. \"If the picture vanishes again, can everyone still find the way out?\"","They turned off the projectors. The ordinary path markers remained bright, and a newly cleared sightline led to the doors. Etta made the staff walk it before serving the cake. A boy tried to submerge a floating wooden boat beneath the last inch of water in the model. His mother stopped him. The engineer smiled and showed him where to place it safely.","\"The physics lesson survives,\" Cassian observed.","\"Practically miraculous,\" Mara said.","Lio waited near the cloakroom. He had returned the borrowed costume, washed and folded. Without the performance coat he looked younger and more exhausted. The actor wanted to speak to Mara, but Cassian did not usher him toward her as if access could be awarded. He asked her with a glance, and she walked over herself.","\"I wanted her to matter,\" Lio said.","\"She already did.\"","\"I know. I think I wanted everyone else to know.\"","Mara understood that hunger. She had once made her mother's disappearance the center of every dangerous choice. Love did not keep a person wise. Sometimes it narrowed the world until one wrong seemed permission for another. She let the thought pass without offering Lio a borrowed cure for grief.","\"Tell your sister what you've learned,\" she said. \"Let her choose how much of her work becomes public.\"","He nodded. At the moment of parting, he handed Etta the old letter, preserving his own copy. \"The original belongs with the study. I should have asked before assuming it was mine to hide.\"","The café lights glowed beyond the hall. Mara unconsciously straightened the collar Cassian had already straightened twice. He caught her hand and kissed the inside of her wrist. \"If you improve me much more, I shall become impossible to live with.\"","\"You were already difficult.\"","\"Then the work is safely complete.\"","She laughed against his shoulder. Etta watched them with the gentle amusement of a woman pleased that at least one scene in her museum required no repair.","When the cake arrived, Cassian divided his slice exactly in half, then moved the larger-looking portion onto Mara's plate. She noticed. She always noticed the small frauds he committed in her favor. The affection could still waken a ridiculous warmth in her after all the larger dramas of their lives.","Outside, the harbor lights shone across real water. The model's storm had stopped; the weather beyond the glass remained free to do as it pleased. Mara took off her shoes before the last step onto the sand. Cassian followed, carrying them without comment. Their walk had no case attached to it, and for once she was happy to let the next mystery wait."],"questions":[],"wordCount":793,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0162\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">婚后外传：Rain in a Bottle</div><h1>The Weather beyond the Glass</h1><p id=\"chapter-0162-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5505\" data-words=\"5505\">next</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-2932\" data-words=\"2932\">evening</a>, Etta <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4543\" data-words=\"4543\">invited</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8426\" data-words=\"8426\">them</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-711\" data-words=\"711\">back</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-231\" data-words=\"231\">after</a> closing. Greenbelt","anchor":"chapter-0163","paragraphs":["June Bell earned her living by asking questions people wished she would forget. At the old automotive-assembly plant, her foremost question was why a municipal contractor had ordered a community theater to close down one day before its reopening. The former factory stood beside the green belt, its pre-bubble machinery preserved around a small stage. Its manager had spent years making the place culturally welcoming. Now a notice called it a deadly hazard.","Mara, dressed as a duchess from the Renaissance, read the paper over June's shoulder. \"In my opinion, we should inquire before we panic.\"","\"In my opinion, your husband needs to see that neckline.\"","Cassian, already looking, declined to calm down on command.","The evening would commence with a playful history of inventions. An esteemed futurologist had supplied an excessively rosy introduction to the future, and a visiting parliamentary group, including two Liberal Democrats, had agreed to attend. The theater used the proceeds for youth workshops. Its counter-culture origins were evident in every odd painted chair. A money-making mega-project across the road wanted the same riverside plot. Its monetary offer was generous; its interest in public performance was less clear.","The factory's demographics had changed. Former machinists now lived beside young families, and the theater's realistic plan was tied to what both groups could afford. Its grant would not be tied to ticket sales alone. A nuclear engineer taught a workshop here; she and a retired tailor had innovatively built props from discarded cases. The mixture was hugely appealing to the children.","Manager Dena Marken was at the brink of surrender. Her office was situated beside the stage. \"They say we can be confined to fifteen people henceforth. That wouldn't pay the light bill.\"","\"Then we'll do our utmost to find the reason,\" Mara said. \"We are open to a genuine safety finding. We're not obliged to accept a forged one.\"","The layout offered no glaring defect. Fire exits had continuous lighting, and the new sound module had an authentic inspection certificate. One carpenter had been hired to ceil the rehearsal room with lightweight panels; the work had been delayed, leaving the structure visible. The inspector could inspect it without tearing anything down. That was a setback to Dena's schedule, yet an advantage now.","June wanted the officials to convene on site. She would report impartially, she promised, despite her inclination to like the theater. A critic's razor-sharp mind was no substitute for checking a suspicion. She had recently written about neurobiology and psychiatry, and learned how easily an attractive inference could outrun its premises. Her objective today was smaller: obtain the original order.","A deputy arrived, claiming the gathering could incite disorder. \"This is basically unconstitutional,\" a volunteer said.","\"That's a legal claim,\" June answered. \"Let's first ask what authority he says he has.\"","The notice's final demand boiled down to immediate surrender of the keys. Its claims about future danger were beside the point unless the inspector supported them. Dena's tendency to believe an official seal had been costly. Mara would not blame her ignorance; the forged design was meant to prevent questions. Its small encoded stamp looked realistic enough to fool an ordinary reader.","The deputy could not explain the page's missing signature. Consequently, Dena refused to cancel until the inspector answered. She did not intend to deter legitimate visitors from complaining. Her tradition of open doors was intrinsic to the place. Nor would she subscribe to the notion that dissent made a venue dangerous. Neglect would have been easier than resistance; she kept walking the exits with Mara.","The official's phone emitted a sharp tone. From the bright lobby he moved to a dim corner. Mara noticed his hand conceal a second notice, rolled tightly. The first was a partial copy. Its projected attendance had been rounded from twenty to two hundred. Her mastery of paint restoration did not establish forgery, but the printer's uneven blue line appeared on both sheets. The deputy had brought his own replacements.","\"May we visualize the actual audience?\" Cassian asked. \"At present, I count seven.\"","June's story might become a sensation, but she would not let that adversely affect her account. Ideally, the missing record would be found before the visitors arrived. The delay had set back their rehearsal markedly; the stage's cheerful projection still showed a welcome to an audience now waiting outside.","The remark was pleasurable enough to make a volunteer laugh. Dena let herself look forward to the show again. Fear had left her fingers numb; she rubbed them together and went to disinfect a spilled drink. The theater could be fertile ground for arguments without becoming chaotic.","The futurologist swept in wearing a stylish silver coat. His grand manner was an invention he took seriously. He predicted that digital performance would triumph over physical stages within a decade. June asked whether his pre-bubble forecasts had been equally certain. He admitted, with a grin, that his current business depended on people forgiving the older ones. His humor improved the outlook without altering the notice.","A motor sounded behind the stage. Dena's assistant had begun to wander into the loading area. Mara followed and found the contractor's crew loosening the old demonstration car from its display. Removing it would damage the new exit rail. The men said the order authorized them to dismantle obstacles. Their paper bore Dena's copied signature.","Mara stepped between the trolley and the door. \"You fail to show the original approval. Nothing moves.\"","The foreman tried to deter her with the possibility of a lawsuit. Cassian came to her side, his voice very quiet. \"Then you'll be capable of explaining that lawsuit with your hands off the trolley.\"","Dena arrived pale and furious. She had never signed. The forgery's implication went beyond a canceled show: the contractor could empty the building and claim the staff had agreed. Its commercial partner's customer loyalty campaign had promised to save the district's heritage. This was an odd way to keep its word.","June began the hunt for the original file. Mara volunteered to inspect the contractor's temporary office, with permission from the site owner. They would not underestimate a mundane printer. The prospect of a tragic closure had become a practical contest. \"You can rise above the drama later,\" June told Cassian. \"For now, keep her skirt clear of the wheels.\"","He looked at Mara. \"My invariable pleasure.\"","Even though she was trying to remain imposing, she smiled."],"questions":[],"wordCount":1073,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0163\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div navigation\"><button data-go=\"161\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"163\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"x023c02","title":"The Car on the Trolley","volume":"伙伴外传：The Theater at the Greenbelt","anchor":"chapter-0164","paragraphs":["The contractor's temporary office was a mega-box on wheels, situated beside the old automotive-assembly loading bay. Its owner had granted access to the project records, but the deputy blocked the door. \"You'll disrupt a lawful closure.\"","\"In my opinion, a lawful closure can survive a question,\" June said. \"Please inquire whether your supervisor agrees.\"","He called her excessively hostile. She suggested that he convene his supervisor and the actual inspector rather than diagnose her tone. The deputy's attempt to incite resentment among the crew failed; they wanted to earn their living, not become part of an odd public scandal. One man said, \"Basically, we were told to remove a car. We are open to hearing that's wrong.\"","Mara approved of his restraint. A crew could be capable of heavy work without being responsible for every order it received. The foreman closed the trolley brake. The glaring weakness in the contractor's account remained Dena's supposed signature. It encoded permission the manager denied giving. June asked everyone to keep their word about leaving the car in place while she checked.","The office held a realistic rendering of apartments overlooking the green belt. On its wall hung a pre-bubble valuation, beside a much higher modern estimate. The monetary difference explained the developer's eagerness. A successful theater would not automatically prevent housing; the plan's real hindrance was a protected public-use condition on the old factory. The contractor wanted a closure certificate to begin an application to remove that condition.","June would report impartially on how the closure was tied to the development proposal. Its glossy tables on local demographics failed to include the people who used the theater. That omission might not have been accidental, but she would not wander into a claim she could not support. The director tried to deter her by threatening to close down the office itself.","\"That might adversely affect their schedule,\" Cassian observed.","\"Ideally, dishonesty would be an inconvenient setback,\" Mara answered.","The printer emitted its familiar whistle. On the desk lay a fresh copy of the unsigned notice. The digital file recorded an edit at nine that morning, after the inspector had left. June did her utmost to preserve the file without letting anyone overwrite it. She took photographs with the owner's consent and requested an official copy. Her reporting would be impartial; it would not be confined to whichever version arrived most quickly.","Through the window, Dena scrubbed a table to disinfect it before the audience entered. The work gave her hands something to do. Closing down the venue would hurt children whose parents could not afford private lessons. The workshop had become fertile ground for their confidence. A former participant now made props professionally; he was foremost among those carrying chairs back inside.","The contractor arrived with the esteemed futurologist, who looked markedly less buoyant. His rosy opening speech had included the developer's promised investment. He had not known that his sponsor planned to close the current theater. The relationship put him at the brink of humiliation. Consequently, he wanted the whole argument postponed until after his appearance.","\"Beside the point,\" Dena said when he mentioned his reputation. \"My building first.\"","Mara took her place beside the desk. Her costume from the Renaissance was not designed for official confrontations, but its splendid sleeves framed the forged signature beautifully. The contractor accused the counter-culture crowd of obstructing progress. Dena reminded him that culturally inclusive art did not preclude sensible repairs. Her objection boiled down to a paper claiming she had authorized something she had refused.","Two visiting Liberal Democrats entered with the parliamentary group. The contractor appealed to their authority. They declined to judge a local dispute and asked the inspector to speak for herself. June liked that answer better than any grand declaration of support. A claim that the order was unconstitutional would need its own assessment; the immediate document could be checked now.","Dena told the shaken crew to calm down before anyone touched the equipment again. The accident would set back the removal for sure. A stylish worker used the pause to secure the loose wheel with a strap, innovatively adapting an ordinary loading aid after the foreman checked it.","The inspector finally arrived, having been directed to the wrong entrance. She had recommended a new railing near the demonstration car, not the closure of the theater. Her report said the stage could open with a barrier around that exhibit. She would approve the temporary arrangement after seeing it. Henceforth, instructions were to come from her office directly, not through the contractor's promotional staff.","A sudden crash interrupted her. The trolley's loose wheel had rolled into a drain cover. The tilted car strained against its strap, and a worker's hand was caught beneath the handle. Mara braced the frame while Cassian and the foreman took its weight. The worker withdrew a numb hand; a medic examined him. The inspector cleared the area and had the remaining movement stopped. It was chaotic for a minute, not a reason for everyone to rush in.","The contractor called the near accident proof that the building was deadly. The inspector looked at the unapproved removal equipment. \"You created this particular hazard.\"","June watched his certainty falter. Her razor-sharp mind had found an unpleasantly simple account, but she would still give him a chance to answer it. Customer loyalty earned with rescue slogans would not repair a falsified order. His public mask had begun to peel.","Outside, a carpenter offered to ceil the rehearsal room properly next week. He would plan the work around the youth class. Dena could visualize that future again. It was not a triumph over every possible obstacle, only the return of a show she had nearly lost. Its intrinsic value did not require her to promise perfect happiness.","\"Will you subscribe to our newsletter?\" she asked the futurologist.","\"After tonight, for sure.\"","Cassian pulled Mara away before the man could commence another forecast. \"We have a performance to watch. I look forward to seeing you sit down.\"","\"A hugely ambitious prediction.\"","He kissed the back of her hand. The silver-coated prophet pretended not to notice."],"questions":[],"wordCount":1022,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0164\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">伙伴外传：The Theater became a stage. Dena had kept the painted conveyor as a backdrop, and a row of mismatched chairs filled the old workshop. The building was situated just beyond the green belt, where evening birds were louder than the distant traffic. Mara stood in the wings, doing her utmost to prevent her skirt from the Renaissance from tangling with a prop bicycle. Cassian watched with the pleased concentration of a man offered his favorite entertainment. Tonight they would have an audience for sure.","\"In my opinion, the bicycle is winning,\" June said.","\"Inquire again after the show,\" Mara answered.","The esteemed futurologist had revised his opening. His prediction that a mega-investment would transform the district had been removed. Instead, he admitted that his rosy account depended on promises he had not investigated. A photograph of the pre-bubble factory appeared behind him, full of workers whose future had once seemed equally certain. It was a culturally sensitive choice without pretending that the past had been perfect.","The small stage meant the actors would be confined to a marked strip while the repair area stayed closed. One boy failed to remember the boundary and had to be called back. A volunteer told him to calm down, then showed him the glaring yellow tape. He did not wander over it again.","He did not make the apology excessively long. The children were waiting to commence their scenes, and his embarrassment was beside the point. Their foremost interest was whether the cardboard machine would behave. The little invention used a hand crank, encoded with painted arrows showing where to turn. Its intrinsic appeal was that everybody could see how it worked. Consequently, a spectator could help when an actor forgot.","June had asked the inspector to convene a short safety briefing before the doors opened. The actual restriction was not confined to a single performance; the car display would remain closed until its railing was repaired. Dena was open to that requirement. Her counter-culture friends could question authority without treating every warning as oppression. One volunteer repeated that the closure order had been unconstitutional, and June asked him to distinguish his opinion from the inspector's documented finding.","The forgery investigation would continue. The contractor's monetary motive was visible in the proposal, but motive alone did not identify every person involved. June had written the story impartially and given the company a clear deadline to answer. It could not deter publication merely by calling the account hostile. Neither would she incite a crowd to punish the crew who had stopped when the facts changed.","A worker came with his daughter, his injured hand bandaged after an examination. Dena gave them seats near the aisle. He had expected to earn his living carrying a car that day, not rescue the theater. \"Odd job,\" he told Mara.","Dena's revised budget was tied to the district's actual demographics. Her realistic plan reserved free seats for those who needed them. She asked a volunteer to disinfect the shared masks after the performance. The work was less picturesque than a protest, and just as necessary.","\"A genuinely odd one.\"","The daughter's laugh was a small, pleasurable reward. She pointed to the program, where her father's name appeared among the people who had secured the display. Dena had promised him a fair account and kept her word.","Two Liberal Democrats sat with the visiting parliamentary group. The program described them as guests, making no claim that their presence endorsed a local development plan. Their assistant had subscribed to the theater's mailing list after seeing the workshop. A useful connection could grow from the evening without turning it into a party advertisement. The setting remained fertile ground for arguments and songs alike.","Backstage, the futurologist asked whether he should delete every prediction from his work. \"That would basically end my profession.\"","\"Try labeling the assumptions,\" June said. \"Let people see what changes the result.\"","His bright coat shone against the dirty wall. He looked like a man at the brink of a revelation, then asked whether the suggestion would fit on a brochure. Mara left them to negotiate the dimensions of intellectual honesty.","In the second scene, a young actor played a man who tried to close down every place where people disagreed with him. His adversary defeated him by opening a bakery next door and inviting everyone inside. The children had written the ending themselves. It boiled down to a gloriously unrealistic supply of buns. The audience laughed, and Dena laughed hardest.","The contractor's notice had called the site deadly. The actual inspector had identified specific repairs, and those would be done. A chaotic evacuation was not required to demonstrate care. Nor did the successful show erase the near accident. Henceforth, the theater would check outside instructions through the named responsible office. It was a modest promise Dena could fulfill.","Mara finally took her seat beside Cassian. She had been hugely impatient to see the final dance, yet found herself watching his face instead. He took her hand and rested it against his knee. The day had included enough formal courage. This quiet claim on her company was the reward she wanted.","\"Still looking forward to the future?\" she whispered.","\"Especially the next ten minutes. You may remain seated.\"","She stole his program and fanned him with it. Onstage, the bicycle failed magnificently. A child shouted the missing line, the actors recovered, and the audience applauded their ingenuity. Nobody needed a perfect forecast to know they wanted the place to stay."],"questions":[],"wordCount":924,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0165\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">伙伴外传：The Theater at the Greenbelt</div><h1>A Future with Buns</h1><p id=\"chapter-0165-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-593\" data-words=\"593\">At</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7442\" data-words=\"7442\">seven</a>, <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-676\" data-words=\"676\">automotive-assembly</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4863\" data-words=\"4863\">line</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-883\" data-words=\"883\">became</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1\" data-words=\"1\">a</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7877\" data-words=\"7877\">stage</a>.
